| [unicorn/no-instanceof-builtins](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-instanceof-builtins.md)                                       | [S01](#s01) **error** [O101](#o101)        | Correctness     | Cross-realm values can fail instanceof, but constructor identity can be the intended question in a controlled realm.                                               |
| [unicorn/no-loop-iterable-mutation](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-loop-iterable-mutation.md)                                 | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Correctness     | Mutation can skip or repeat visits, but some worklist algorithms intentionally rely on the collection's documented traversal semantics.                            |
| [unicorn/no-magic-array-flat-depth](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-magic-array-flat-depth.md)                                 | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | A named depth can explain expected nesting, but a small literal depth may be clearer than an artificial constant.                                                  |
| [unicorn/no-negated-array-predicate](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-negated-array-predicate.md)                               | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Equivalent positive some/every forms can simplify conditions, but De Morgan rewrites may make the domain question less natural.                                    |
| [unicorn/no-negated-comparison](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-negated-comparison.md)                                         | [S01](#s01) **error** [O102](#o102)        | Readability     | A direct opposite comparison can be clearer, but NaN and type semantics require care and negative conditions can express intent.                                   |
| [unicorn/no-negated-condition](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-negated-condition.md)                                           | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Positive condition order can ease reading, but negative guards often make exceptional paths clearer.                                                               |
| [unicorn/no-nested-ternary](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-nested-ternary.md)                                                 | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Branch blocks can clarify complex alternatives, but short structured ternaries can present a small value mapping more clearly.                                     |
| [unicorn/no-non-function-verb-prefix](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-non-function-verb-prefix.md)                             | [S01](#s01) **error** [O103](#o103)        | Readability     | Action names can signal callability, but derived domain values can legitimately use established verb-like names.                                                   |
| [unicorn/no-object-as-default-parameter](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-object-as-default-parameter.md)                       | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Correctness     | Per-field defaults prevent partial option objects from losing defaults, but a whole-object default can be the intentional all-or-nothing contract.                 |
| [unicorn/no-process-exit](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-process-exit.md)                                                     | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Lifecycle       | Avoids terminating before pending writes and cleanup finish, but deliberate CLI termination can be valid after explicit cleanup.                                   |
| [unicorn/no-repeated-call-wrappers](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-repeated-call-wrappers.md)                                 | [S01](#s01) **warn** [O000](#o000)         | Maintainability | A helper can centralize repeated wrapping, but a small number of explicit calls can be clearer than another abstraction.                                           |
| [unicorn/no-single-promise-in-promise-methods](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-single-promise-in-promise-methods.md)           | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | A direct promise can be simpler, but aggregate methods can intentionally change result shape or rejection semantics even with one member.                          |
| [unicorn/no-static-only-class](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-static-only-class.md)                                           | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Maintainability | Module functions avoid unnecessary class scaffolding, but framework registries and public API compatibility can justify static containers.                         |
| [unicorn/no-thenable](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-thenable.md)                                                             | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Correctness     | Accidental then properties trigger Promise assimilation, but deliberate thenable implementations are legitimate interoperable APIs.                                |
| [unicorn/no-this-assignment](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-this-assignment.md)                                               | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Lexical arrows often express receiver capture clearly, but explicit aliases can distinguish outer and inner receivers.                                             |
| [unicorn/no-this-outside-of-class](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-this-outside-of-class.md)                                   | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Restricting this narrows receiver ambiguity, but ordinary functions and host callback protocols can intentionally bind this.                                       |
| [unicorn/no-top-level-assignment-in-function](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-top-level-assignment-in-function.md)             | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Ownership       | Makes hidden module-state changes visible for review, but explicit caches and stateful services legitimately update module bindings.                               |
| [unicorn/no-top-level-side-effects](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-top-level-side-effects.md)                                 | [S01](#s01) **error** [O104](#o104)        | Maintainability | Pure module loading simplifies reuse and testing, but registration modules and entry points can intentionally perform initialization.                              |
| [unicorn/no-unnecessary-await](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-unnecessary-await.md)                                           | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Removes misleading asynchronous boundaries, but await can deliberately yield a microtask or accommodate an evolving sync/async contract.                           |
| [unicorn/no-unnecessary-fetch-options](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-unnecessary-fetch-options.md)                           | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Removing defaults highlights exceptional request behavior, but explicit defaults can document security or protocol assumptions.                                    |
| [unicorn/no-unnecessary-parameters](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-unnecessary-parameters.md)                                 | [S01](#s01) **error** [O106](#o106)        | Maintainability | Invariant private parameters add fake variability, but future extension and public callback contracts can require them.                                            |
| [unicorn/no-unnecessary-polyfills](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-unnecessary-polyfills.md)                                   | [S01](#s01) **error** [O035](#o035)        | Maintainability | Native APIs avoid maintaining duplicate algorithms, but actual deployment support must be checked before removing compatibility code.                              |
| [unicorn/no-unnecessary-string-trim](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-unnecessary-string-trim.md)                               | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Performance     | Avoids trimming that cannot affect the specific prefix/suffix check, but readability and exact whitespace intent need semantic verification.                       |
| [unicorn/no-unreadable-for-of-expression](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-unreadable-for-of-expression.md)                     | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Naming a complex iterable separates construction from traversal, but extracting short obvious expressions can add clutter.                                         |
| [unicorn/no-unreadable-iife](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-unreadable-iife.md)                                               | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Can expose hidden execution inside expressions, but an IIFE can cleanly delimit a one-use initialization scope.                                                    |
| [unicorn/no-unreadable-object-destructuring](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-unreadable-object-destructuring.md)               | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Splitting complex binding patterns improves local comprehension, but modest nested destructuring can state dependencies compactly.                                 |
| [unicorn/no-useless-coercion](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-useless-coercion.md)                                             | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Avoids redundant conversion layers, but explicit normalization can communicate boundary intent when static assumptions are incomplete.                             |
| [unicorn/no-useless-delete-check](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-useless-delete-check.md)                                     | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Deletion usually handles absent properties directly, but proxy traps or inherited-key checks can make the guard meaningful.                                        |
| [unicorn/no-useless-else](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-useless-else.md)                                                     | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Removing else after an exit reduces nesting, but symmetric alternatives can be easier to compare inside one explicit branch structure.                             |
| [unicorn/no-useless-error-capture-stack-trace](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-useless-error-capture-stack-trace.md)           | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Maintainability | Avoids duplicate stack-capture boilerplate, but customized error stacks may deliberately need a particular capture boundary.                                       |
| [unicorn/no-useless-length-check](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-useless-length-check.md)                                     | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Removes guards for operations that naturally handle empty collections, but explicit emptiness branches can document a domain boundary.                             |
| [unicorn/no-useless-promise-resolve-reject](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-useless-promise-resolve-reject.md)                 | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Direct returns/throws clarify many async paths, but promise assimilation, microtask timing, and error boundaries need preservation.                                |
| [unicorn/no-useless-re-export](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-useless-re-export.md)                                           | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Maintainability | Avoids redundant indirection, but reexports can intentionally define a stable public module boundary.                                                              |
| [unicorn/no-useless-recursion](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-useless-recursion.md)                                           | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Maintainability | Loops can avoid unnecessary stack growth, but recursive notation can better match a small recursive data contract.                                                 |
| [unicorn/no-useless-undefined](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/no-useless-undefined.md)                                           | [S01](#s01) **error** [O108](#o108)        | Readability     | Omitting redundant undefined can shorten code, but explicit absent results sometimes clarify an API contract.                                                      |
| [unicorn/numeric-separators-style](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/numeric-separators-style.md)                                   | [S01](#s01) **error** [O109](#o109)        | Readability     | Grouped digits can make magnitudes easier to verify, but mandated grouping can conflict with domain-specific number structure.                                     |
| [unicorn/operator-assignment](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/operator-assignment.md)                                             | [S01](#s01) **error** [O003](#o003)        | Readability     | Compound assignment highlights updating one target, but expanded arithmetic can be clearer in formulas and repeated access may have effects.                       |
| [unicorn/prefer-abort-signal-timeout](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-abort-signal-timeout.md)                             | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Lifecycle       | Native timeout signals reduce timer boilerplate, but cancellation control, reason, and runtime support may require a manual controller.                            |
| [unicorn/prefer-add-event-listener](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-add-event-listener.md)                                 | [S01](#s01) **error** [O110](#o110)        | Lifecycle       | Listener APIs support independent handlers and removal, but assigning an event property can intentionally express single-handler ownership.                        |
| [unicorn/prefer-array-flat-map](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-array-flat-map.md)                                         | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Performance     | Combining map and flatten can avoid an intermediate array, but fused expressions can obscure distinct transformation stages.                                       |
| [unicorn/prefer-array-from-async](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-array-from-async.md)                                     | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Native async collection can simplify accumulation, but ordering, concurrency, cancellation, and runtime support must match the original loop.                      |
| [unicorn/prefer-array-from-map](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-array-from-map.md)                                         | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Performance     | Mapping during Array.from avoids an intermediate array, but a separate map can better expose transformation stages.                                                |
| [unicorn/prefer-at](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-at.md)                                                                 | [S01](#s01) **error** [O113](#o113)        | Readability     | Negative indexing can clarify tail access, but ordinary positive bracket indexing is familiar and does not need method-call conversion.                            |
| [unicorn/prefer-await](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-await.md)                                                           | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Await often exposes sequential control flow, but promise composition can better express concurrent pipelines and deliberate detached work.                         |
| [unicorn/prefer-bigint-literals](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-bigint-literals.md)                                       | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Correctness     | BigInt literals avoid converting an already rounded Number, but equivalent string construction can be clearer for dynamic or serialized input.                     |
| [unicorn/prefer-block-statement-over-iife](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-block-statement-over-iife.md)                   | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | A block avoids a needless call when only scope is needed, but expression-valued initialization still legitimately needs another construct.                         |
| [unicorn/prefer-class-fields](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-class-fields.md)                                             | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Fields make instance shape visible, but initialization order relative to super and setters can differ from constructor assignments.                                |
| [unicorn/prefer-code-point](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-code-point.md)                                                 | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Correctness     | Code-point APIs avoid splitting surrogate pairs, but protocols that intentionally operate on UTF-16 code units require the older operations.                       |
| [unicorn/prefer-combined-guards](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-combined-guards.md)                                       | [S01](#s01) **error** [O114](#o114)        | Readability     | Combining identical exits reduces duplicated branches, but separate guards may document independent validation failures more clearly.                              |
| [unicorn/prefer-continue](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-continue.md)                                                     | [S01](#s01) **error** [O115](#o115)        | Readability     | Early continues flatten the main loop body, but many scattered exits can make a short traversal harder to follow.                                                  |
| [unicorn/prefer-default-parameters](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-default-parameters.md)                                 | [S01](#s01) **error** [O116](#o116)        | Readability     | Signature defaults expose optional inputs, but default parameters and truthy fallbacks differ for null and other falsy values.                                     |
| [unicorn/prefer-dom-node-append](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-dom-node-append.md)                                       | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Append offers a simpler multi-node/text API, but appendChild's return value and older support can be part of the contract.                                         |
| [unicorn/prefer-dom-node-html-methods](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-dom-node-html-methods.md)                           | [S01](#s01) **error** [O117](#o117)        | Security        | Modern HTML methods can improve sanitization and shadow-root handling, but support and exact parsing semantics must fit the target platform.                       |
| [unicorn/prefer-dom-node-text-content](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-dom-node-text-content.md)                           | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Performance     | TextContent avoids layout-dependent reads, but innerText intentionally reflects rendered visibility and line breaks.                                               |
| [unicorn/prefer-early-return](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-early-return.md)                                             | [S01](#s01) **error** [O115](#o115)        | Readability     | Early guards reduce nesting around the main path, but multiple exits can obscure cleanup or closely related alternatives.                                          |
| [unicorn/prefer-event-target](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-event-target.md)                                             | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Portability     | A standard event interface can reduce platform coupling, but EventEmitter has different error, payload, and listener semantics.                                    |
| [unicorn/prefer-export-from](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-export-from.md)                                               | [S01](#s01) **error** [O118](#o118)        | Readability     | Direct reexports remove unused local bindings, but an explicit local import can document use or support a stable facade.                                           |
| [unicorn/prefer-global-this](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-global-this.md)                                               | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Portability     | A standard global reference supports shared runtimes, but window can deliberately signal browser-only behavior.                                                    |
| [unicorn/prefer-group-by](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-group-by.md)                                                     | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Native grouping names the operation directly, but key types, output prototype, and runtime support can favor an explicit reducer.                                  |
| [unicorn/prefer-hoisting-branch-code](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-hoisting-branch-code.md)                             | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Maintainability | One shared operation prevents branch copies drifting, but moving it across conditions can alter effects and error timing.                                          |
| [unicorn/prefer-includes-over-repeated-comparisons](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-includes-over-repeated-comparisons.md) | [S01](#s01) **error** [O119](#o119)        | Readability     | A membership list can expose allowed values, but constructing arrays for a couple of comparisons can be noisier and allocate unnecessarily.                        |
| [unicorn/prefer-iterator-helpers](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-iterator-helpers.md)                                     | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Performance     | Lazy helpers can avoid intermediate arrays, but iteration timing and newer runtime requirements must match the application.                                        |
| [unicorn/prefer-iterator-zip](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-iterator-zip.md)                                             | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Zip exposes parallel traversal, but unequal-length semantics and runtime availability can differ from explicit indexing.                                           |
| [unicorn/prefer-literal-ascii](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-literal-ascii.md)                                           | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Printable literals are usually easier to read than numeric escapes, but explicit escapes can document a byte-level protocol.                                       |
| [unicorn/prefer-logical-operator-over-ternary](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-logical-operator-over-ternary.md)           | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Logical defaults can simplify equivalent branches, but explicit ternaries may better expose falsy-value semantics.                                                 |
| [unicorn/prefer-map-from-entries](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-map-from-entries.md)                                     | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Correctness     | Map preserves key identity for map-like use, but changing representation can alter serialization, property access, and public contracts.                           |
| [unicorn/prefer-minimal-ternary](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-minimal-ternary.md)                                       | [S01](#s01) **error** [O120](#o120)        | Readability     | Factoring common structure exposes what varies, but deeply embedded ternaries can be harder to read than complete branches.                                        |
| [unicorn/prefer-modern-dom-apis](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-modern-dom-apis.md)                                       | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Compatibility   | Supported modern APIs can reduce legacy workarounds, but old deployment targets or differing collection semantics can justify older methods.                       |
| [unicorn/prefer-module](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-module.md)                                                         | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Maintainability | ES modules expose static dependencies, but CommonJS remains a valid runtime and packaging contract, including files explicitly configured here as CommonJS.        |
| [unicorn/prefer-native-coercion-functions](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-native-coercion-functions.md)                   | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Direct conversion functions avoid trivial wrappers, but callbacks must accept the iterator's extra arguments safely.                                               |
| [unicorn/prefer-number-coercion](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-number-coercion.md)                                       | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Correctness     | Number requires a whole numeric string, which can prevent partial parsing, but parseInt/parseFloat may intentionally accept prefixes.                              |
| [unicorn/prefer-object-define-properties](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-object-define-properties.md)                     | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | A single descriptor object groups initialization, but separate definitions can reflect dependencies or deliberate intermediate states.                             |
| [unicorn/prefer-observer-apis](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-observer-apis.md)                                           | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Performance     | Observers can avoid polling layout during scroll/resize events, but delivery timing and support can differ from the required interaction.                          |
| [unicorn/prefer-path2d](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-path2d.md)                                                         | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Performance     | Reusing stable canvas paths can reduce repeated setup, but dynamic paths and small drawings may not justify caching.                                               |

| [unicorn/prefer-query-selector](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-query-selector.md)                                         | [S01](#s01) **error** [O122](#o122)        | Readability     | A common selector API reduces vocabulary, but live collections, escaping needs, and simple ID lookup can favor older APIs.                                         |

| [unicorn/prefer-set-has](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-set-has.md)                                                       | [S01](#s01) **error** [O124](#o124)        | Performance     | Repeated membership can benefit from a Set, but construction, retention, and small collections can outweigh lookup savings.                                        |

| [unicorn/prefer-single-object-destructuring](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-single-object-destructuring.md)               | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | One binding pattern shows required fields together, but grouping unrelated reads can reduce locality or complicate declarations.                                   |

| [unicorn/prefer-string-slice](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-string-slice.md)                                             | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Compatibility   | Slice avoids deprecated substr and has consistent indexing, but substring's clamping/swapping behavior can be intentional.                                         |

| [unicorn/prefer-switch](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-switch.md)                                                         | [S01](#s01) **error** [O127](#o127)        | Readability     | Switch can organize many discrete alternatives, but predicates and small condition chains can remain clearer as if/else.                                           |
| [unicorn/prefer-ternary](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-ternary.md)                                                       | [S01](#s01) **error** [O128](#o128)        | Readability     | A ternary can express a small value choice, but forcing it can compress distinct actions into a harder-to-read expression.                                         |

| [unicorn/prefer-top-level-await](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-top-level-await.md)                                       | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Top-level await can clarify module initialization, but it changes loading constraints and may not fit CommonJS or application startup ownership.                   |

| [unicorn/prefer-url-href](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-url-href.md)                                                     | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Href explicitly names the serialized URL, but String(url) already has a well-defined and familiar contract.                                                        |
| [unicorn/prefer-while-loop-condition](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/prefer-while-loop-condition.md)                             | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | A visible loop condition explains continuation, but an internal break can naturally follow work needed to compute the exit.                                        |
| [unicorn/require-array-join-separator](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/require-array-join-separator.md)                           | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | An explicit separator exposes output format, but repeating the documented comma default adds little for obvious local use.                                         |
| [unicorn/require-module-attributes](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/require-module-attributes.md)                                 | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Compatibility   | Explicit import attributes can document loader expectations, but required attribute syntax depends on module type and runtime support.                             |
| [unicorn/require-module-specifiers](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/require-module-specifiers.md)                                 | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Maintainability | Nonempty specifiers avoid misleading empty imports/exports, but empty exports intentionally mark some TypeScript files as modules.                                 |
| [unicorn/require-number-to-fixed-digits-argument](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/require-number-to-fixed-digits-argument.md)     | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | Explicit precision documents rounding, but repeating a clear zero default is often noise.                                                                          |
| [unicorn/require-passive-events](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/require-passive-events.md)                                       | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Performance     | Passive listeners avoid blocking scroll when cancellation is unnecessary, but gesture handlers that must cancel need nonpassive behavior.                          |
| [unicorn/switch-case-braces](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/switch-case-braces.md)                                               | [S01](#s01) **error** [O003](#o003)        | Correctness     | Case blocks can isolate lexical bindings, but mandatory braces on cases without declarations add structure without that benefit.                                   |
| [unicorn/throw-new-error](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/throw-new-error.md)                                                     | [S01](#s01) **error** [O000](#o000)        | Readability     | New makes construction visually explicit, but built-in Error calls behave equivalently and custom factory functions may differ.                                    |
| [unicorn/try-complexity](https://github.com/sindresorhus/eslint-plugin-unicorn/blob/v78.0.0/docs/rules/try-complexity.md)                                                       | [S01](#s01) **warn** [O021](#o021)         | Maintainability | Small protected regions can clarify which failures share handling, but a branch-free threshold of one can force helpers that hide the real transaction.            |

### react-doctor lint rules

153 entries. Configuration source: [`eslint.config.ts`](https://github.com/jdeurt/agents/blob/50afb8a256431fce31acd2617590925c298845d2/eslint.config.ts); rule links point to primary documentation supplied by the locked implementation.

| ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| [react-doctor/advanced-event-handler-refs](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/advanced-event-handler-refs)                                                   | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Performance     | Stable listeners can avoid resubscription, but ref indirection complicates reasoning and resubscription may be cheap or intentional.                        |

| [react-doctor/async-await-in-loop](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/async-await-in-loop)                                                                   | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N                                                                                                                                     | Concurrency     | Independent work may overlap safely, but sequential loops can encode ordering, rate limits, transactions, or failure boundaries.                            |
| [react-doctor/async-defer-await](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/async-defer-await)                                                                       | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Performance     | Moving work after guards can avoid wasted waiting, but the operation may intentionally perform effects before an early return.                              |
| [react-doctor/async-parallel](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/async-parallel)                                                                             | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N                                                                                                                                     | Concurrency     | Concurrent independent operations can reduce serial delay, but syntactic independence does not prove resource or failure independence.                      |
| [react-doctor/client-passive-event-listeners](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/client-passive-event-listeners)                                             | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Performance     | Passive listeners can keep scrolling responsive, but handlers that cancel gestures must retain nonpassive semantics.                                        |
| [react-doctor/context-provider-value-from-unmemoized-local-literal](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/context-provider-value-from-unmemoized-local-literal) | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; J; target: react; disabledWhen: react-compiler                                                                                     | Performance     | Stable context values can avoid unrelated consumer updates, but memoization overhead and intentional propagation depend on the component.                   |
| [react-doctor/iframe-missing-sandbox](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/iframe-missing-sandbox)                                                             | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>target: react                                                                                                                         | Security        | Sandboxing can restrict untrusted embeds, but trusted integrations need deliberate capabilities and a blanket requirement does not select safe permissions. |
| [react-doctor/img-redundant-alt](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/img-redundant-alt)                                                                       | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>J; target: react                                                                                                                      | Accessibility   | Removing redundant image announcements can improve listening, but words such as photo can meaningfully distinguish medium or context.                       |

| [react-doctor/js-batch-dom-css](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/js-batch-dom-css)                                                                         | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N                                                                                                                                     | Performance     | Separating layout reads and writes can avoid repeated recalculation, but batching must preserve intermediate measurements and visible timing.               |
| [react-doctor/js-cache-storage](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/js-cache-storage)                                                                         | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N                                                                                                                                     | Performance     | Reusing storage reads can avoid repeated synchronous work, but cached values may become stale across tabs or writers.                                       |
| [react-doctor/js-hoist-intl](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/js-hoist-intl)                                                                               | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N                                                                                                                                     | Performance     | Reusing expensive formatters can help hot paths, but locale/options lifetime and actual call frequency determine the benefit.                               |

| [react-doctor/js-index-maps](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/js-index-maps)                                                                               | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N                                                                                                                                     | Performance     | An index can avoid repeated scans, but construction cost, duplicate-key semantics, and first-match behavior can favor find().                               |
| [react-doctor/js-set-map-lookups](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/js-set-map-lookups)                                                                     | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N                                                                                                                                     | Performance     | Repeated membership may benefit from indexing, but small collections and infrequent queries may not repay setup and retention costs.                        |

| [react-doctor/jsx-no-jsx-as-prop](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/jsx-no-jsx-as-prop)                                                                     | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>J; target: react; disabledWhen: react-compiler                                                                                        | Performance     | Stable JSX props can preserve memoized children, but composition through JSX props is idiomatic and often needs no caching.                                 |
| [react-doctor/jsx-no-new-array-as-prop](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/jsx-no-new-array-as-prop)                                                         | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>J; target: react; disabledWhen: react-compiler                                                                                        | Performance     | Stable array identity can help memoized consumers, but allocating small props is often cheaper and clearer than memoization.                                |
| [react-doctor/jsx-no-new-function-as-prop](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/jsx-no-new-function-as-prop)                                                   | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>J; target: react; disabledWhen: react-compiler                                                                                        | Performance     | Stable callbacks can preserve memoization, but blanket useCallback adds dependencies and complexity without a proven sensitive consumer.                    |

| [react-doctor/media-has-caption](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/media-has-caption)                                                                       | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>J; target: react                                                                                                                      | Accessibility   | Captions provide access to speech content, but silent media and alternative presentations require context beyond a universal track check.                   |
| [react-doctor/no-access-key](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-access-key)                                                                               | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>J; target: react                                                                                                                      | Accessibility   | Avoids conflicts with browser and assistive shortcuts, but a deliberately designed application shortcut can have user value.                                |
| [react-doctor/no-adjust-state-on-prop-change](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-adjust-state-on-prop-change)                                             | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Maintainability | Deriving data can remove synchronization loops, but editable drafts and deliberate prop-change resets can require local state.                              |
| [react-doctor/no-array-index-as-key](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-array-index-as-key)                                                               | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)                                                                                                                                          | Correctness     | Stable item IDs preserve state through reordering, but index keys are valid for truly static position-based lists.                                          |
| [react-doctor/no-autofocus](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-autofocus)                                                                                 | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>J; target: react                                                                                                                      | Accessibility   | Avoids unexpected focus theft on load, but deliberate autofocus can improve modal or single-task workflows.                                                 |
| [react-doctor/no-chain-state-updates](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-chain-state-updates)                                                             | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react; disabledWhen: react:18                                                                                              | Maintainability | One deliberate transition can replace fragile effect chains, but staged external synchronization can legitimately require separate effects.                 |
| [react-doctor/no-derived-state](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-derived-state)                                                                         | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Maintainability | Derivation can remove stale duplicated state, but snapshots and editable drafts can intentionally diverge from current props.                               |
| [react-doctor/no-derived-state-effect](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-derived-state-effect)                                                           | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Maintainability | Computing from current inputs avoids an extra stale render, but synchronization with external systems can require effects.                                  |
| [react-doctor/no-derived-useState](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-derived-useState)                                                                   | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Correctness     | Flags initial prop copies that never update, but initial-only defaults can be a deliberate uncontrolled-component contract.                                 |
| [react-doctor/no-did-mount-set-state](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-did-mount-set-state)                                                             | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>target: react                                                                                                                         | Performance     | Avoids an extra mount render when state is already known, but measurement and external initialization often legitimately occur after mount.                 |
| [react-doctor/no-did-update-set-state](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-did-update-set-state)                                                           | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>target: react                                                                                                                         | Correctness     | Highlights potential update loops, but guarded reconciliation in legacy components can require a post-update state change.                                  |
| [react-doctor/no-document-start-view-transition](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-document-start-view-transition)                                       | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Lifecycle       | React-owned transitions can coordinate rendering, but direct platform transitions can be correct outside React ownership or without that feature.           |
| [react-doctor/no-dynamic-import-path](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-dynamic-import-path)                                                             | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N                                                                                                                                     | Compatibility   | Static import paths help bundlers discover chunks, but supported glob patterns and runtime module loaders can require dynamic paths.                        |
| [react-doctor/no-effect-chain](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-effect-chain)                                                                           | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Maintainability | Combining dependent transitions can reduce intermediate states, but distinct external lifecycles may warrant separate effects.                              |
| [react-doctor/no-effect-event-handler](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-effect-event-handler)                                                           | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Maintainability | Direct event handling makes cause and effect visible, but some effects intentionally react to state changes from multiple sources.                          |
| [react-doctor/no-event-handler](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-event-handler)                                                                         | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Maintainability | Placing event effects with their trigger clarifies ownership, but state-driven synchronization can be appropriate when no single event owns it.             |
| [react-doctor/no-event-trigger-state](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-event-trigger-state)                                                             | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Maintainability | Removing state used only as a trigger simplifies flow, but queued or cross-component commands can require explicit durable state.                           |
| [react-doctor/no-fetch-in-effect](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-fetch-in-effect)                                                                     | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>target: react                                                                                                                         | Maintainability | A data layer can centralize caching and cancellation, but a small correctly cleaned-up effect may be the simplest valid implementation.                     |
| [react-doctor/no-flush-sync](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-flush-sync)                                                                               | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Performance     | Avoids unnecessary synchronous rendering, but imperative host integrations can require a completed DOM update before the next operation.                    |
| [react-doctor/no-full-lodash-import](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-full-lodash-import)                                                               | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; I: `lodash`                                                                                                                        | Performance     | Narrow imports can reduce bundled code, but actual savings depend on module format and bundler tree shaking.                                                |
| [react-doctor/no-giant-component](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-giant-component)                                                                     | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; J                                                                                                                                  | Maintainability | Large components merit decomposition review, but line count does not establish incohesion and extraction can scatter related logic.                         |
| [react-doctor/no-global-css-variable-animation](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-global-css-variable-animation)                                         | [S09](#s09) **error** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                     | Performance     | Local animated variables can limit style recalculation, but a deliberately global animation may require coordinated updates.                                |
| [react-doctor/no-high-complexity-react-function](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-high-complexity-react-function)                                       | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; J                                                                                                                                  | Maintainability | Branch counts reveal dense UI logic, but thresholds cannot distinguish clear domain alternatives from tangled implementation.                               |
| [react-doctor/no-img-lazy-with-high-fetchpriority](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-img-lazy-with-high-fetchpriority)                                   | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>target: react                                                                                                                         | Performance     | Flags conflicting loading intent, but priority within deferred images can be deliberate and does not itself establish a defect.                             |
| [react-doctor/no-initialize-state](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-initialize-state)                                                                   | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Performance     | Initial state can avoid a blank first render, but browser-only inputs and post-mount measurements may require delayed initialization.                       |
| [react-doctor/no-inline-hoc-on-component](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-inline-hoc-on-component)                                                     | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; J                                                                                                                                  | Maintainability | Named component boundaries can preserve lint visibility and diagnostics, but stable module-scope inline HOC arguments need not harm behavior.               |
| [react-doctor/no-inline-prop-on-memo-component](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-inline-prop-on-memo-component)                                         | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react; disabledWhen: react-compiler                                                                                        | Performance     | Stable props can let memoization work, but callback dependencies and actual render cost determine whether added caching is worthwhile.                      |
| [react-doctor/no-is-mounted](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-is-mounted)                                                                               | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>target: react                                                                                                                         | Lifecycle       | Cancellation usually gives clearer async ownership, but legacy migration constraints require more care than replacing one mounted-state check mechanically. |
| [react-doctor/no-json-parse-stringify-clone](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-json-parse-stringify-clone)                                               | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>disabledWhen: react-native                                                                                                            | Correctness     | Flags type loss in JSON cloning, but JSON normalization may be intentional and structuredClone does not preserve every value either.                        |
| [react-doctor/no-large-animated-blur](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-large-animated-blur)                                                             | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Performance     | Large animated blur can strain rendering, but a fixed radius threshold ignores element size, device capability, and visual requirements.                    |
| [react-doctor/no-layout-property-animation](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-layout-property-animation)                                                 | [S09](#s09) **error** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                     | Performance     | Transform-based motion can avoid layout work, but real layout changes sometimes are the intended animation.                                                 |
| [react-doctor/no-legacy-class-lifecycles](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-legacy-class-lifecycles)                                                     | [S09](#s09) **error** [O000](#o000)<br>target: react                                                                                                                        | Compatibility   | Safer lifecycle placement can reduce replay hazards, but migration must preserve timing and match the installed React version.                              |
| [react-doctor/no-mirror-prop-effect](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-mirror-prop-effect)                                                               | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Maintainability | Direct prop reads avoid stale copied state, but a deliberate local draft can require a controlled synchronization policy.                                   |
| [react-doctor/no-moment](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-moment)                                                                                       | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; I: `moment`                                                                                                                        | Performance     | Replacing a large legacy date library may reduce payload, but timezone semantics, compatibility, and migration cost require evidence.                       |
| [react-doctor/no-mutate-queried-dom-node-in-component](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-mutate-queried-dom-node-in-component)                           | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)                                                                                                                                          | Ownership       | Declarative updates preserve React's DOM ownership, but refs and controlled imperative integrations can intentionally manage specific properties.           |
| [react-doctor/no-noninteractive-element-to-interactive-role](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-noninteractive-element-to-interactive-role)               | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>J; target: react                                                                                                                      | Accessibility   | Native controls provide behavior automatically, but a fully implemented ARIA widget can legitimately assign an interactive role.                            |
| [react-doctor/no-noninteractive-tabindex](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-noninteractive-tabindex)                                                     | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>J; target: react                                                                                                                      | Accessibility   | Avoids unnecessary keyboard stops, but focusable reading regions and custom interaction patterns can be intentional.                                        |
| [react-doctor/no-pass-data-to-parent](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-pass-data-to-parent)                                                             | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Maintainability | Upstream ownership can remove effect-based data relays, but reusable child abstractions sometimes legitimately report results upward.                       |
| [react-doctor/no-pass-live-state-to-parent](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-pass-live-state-to-parent)                                                 | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Maintainability | One state owner can avoid duplicated synchronization, but controlled adapters can require explicit upstream notifications.                                  |
| [react-doctor/no-permanent-will-change](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-permanent-will-change)                                                         | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Performance     | Temporary hints can avoid retaining unnecessary compositor resources, but persistent animation surfaces may deliberately keep the hint.                     |
| [react-doctor/no-prevent-default](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-prevent-default)                                                                     | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N                                                                                                                                     | Accessibility   | Native navigation/submission can preserve browser behavior, but client routing and form libraries legitimately cancel default actions.                      |
| [react-doctor/no-prop-callback-in-effect](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-prop-callback-in-effect)                                                     | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Maintainability | Shared ownership can replace indirect effect notifications, but external synchronization and reusable adapters may need callback effects.                   |
| [react-doctor/no-render-in-render](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-render-in-render)                                                                   | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N                                                                                                                                     | Maintainability | A named component can expose state ownership, but plain render helpers without hooks or identity obligations can be clear and valid.                        |
| [react-doctor/no-reset-all-state-on-prop-change](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-reset-all-state-on-prop-change)                                       | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Maintainability | Keyed ownership can simplify complete resets, but remounting also resets resources and focus that an explicit state reset may preserve.                     |
| [react-doctor/no-srcset-without-sizes](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-srcset-without-sizes)                                                           | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>target: react                                                                                                                         | Performance     | Accurate sizes help responsive resource selection, but density-based srcSet and default full-width layouts may not need an explicit value.                  |
| [react-doctor/no-static-motion-config-never](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-static-motion-config-never)                                               | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>I: `framer-motion`, `framer-motion/client`, `framer-motion/m`, `motion/react`, `motion/react-client`, `motion/react-m`; target: react | Accessibility   | Respecting reduced motion protects user preferences, but an application may expose an explicit alternative motion-control policy.                           |
| [react-doctor/no-undeferred-third-party](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-undeferred-third-party)                                                       | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N                                                                                                                                     | Performance     | Deferring nonessential scripts can improve startup, but scripts with required ordering or pre-render work cannot always be delayed.                         |
| [react-doctor/no-unsafe](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-unsafe)                                                                                       | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>target: react                                                                                                                         | Compatibility   | Flags lifecycle methods unsafe under modern rendering, but migration requires version-aware timing changes rather than mechanical renaming.                 |
| [react-doctor/no-unthrottled-scroll-mutation](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-unthrottled-scroll-mutation)                                             | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>target: react                                                                                                                         | Performance     | Limiting scroll-driven writes can avoid excessive layout work, but precise visual tracking may require carefully scheduled frequent updates.                |
| [react-doctor/no-whole-object-dep-with-member-reads](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/no-whole-object-dep-with-member-reads)                               | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react                                                                                                                      | Performance     | Narrow dependencies avoid updates from unrelated fields, but whole-object identity can intentionally represent a versioned input contract.                  |
| [react-doctor/only-export-components](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/only-export-components)                                                             | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>target: react                                                                                                                         | Tooling         | Component-only files can preserve Fast Refresh state, but colocated constants are useful and supported export patterns vary by framework.                   |
| [react-doctor/prefer-dynamic-import](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/prefer-dynamic-import)                                                               | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N                                                                                                                                     | Performance     | Deferred loading can reduce initial payload, but added waterfalls and loading states can worsen frequently used paths.                                      |
| [react-doctor/prefer-html-dialog](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/prefer-html-dialog)                                                                     | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>target: react                                                                                                                         | Accessibility   | Native dialogs supply useful focus and modal behavior, but existing accessible primitives and platform support may justify custom implementations.          |
| [react-doctor/prefer-stable-empty-fallback](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/prefer-stable-empty-fallback)                                                 | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; J; target: react; disabledWhen: react-compiler                                                                                     | Performance     | Stable empty values can preserve memoization, but shared mutable fallbacks introduce ownership risk and small local values may be harmless.                 |
| [react-doctor/prefer-use-effect-event](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/prefer-use-effect-event)                                                           | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>N; target: react, react:19.2                                                                                                          | Lifecycle       | Effect Events can preserve subscription identity while reading current values, but must not hide real reactive dependencies and require supported React.    |
| [react-doctor/r3f-cap-device-pixel-ratio](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/r3f-cap-device-pixel-ratio)                                                     | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>J; target: react, r3f; constructs: r3f, webgl                                                                                         | Performance     | Bounding pixel ratio limits rendering cost, but an arbitrary cap trades visual fidelity for speed without measuring the target device.                      |
| [react-doctor/r3f-limit-shadowed-point-lights](https://react.doctor/docs/rules/react-doctor/r3f-limit-shadowed-point-lights)                                           | [S09](#s09) **warn** [O000](#o000)<br>J; target: react, r3f; constructs: r3f, webgl                                                                                         | Performance     | Point-light shadows multiply rendering work, but a fixed light count ignores scene complexity and the intended visual result.                               |
